<div class="mini-widgets"><div class="text-area in-menu-on-mobile"><h1 class="mission-header"><span>Our Mission</span></h1>
<div class="mission-header">
<p>The mission of the Rockford Public Schools is to provide a safe teaching and learning environment which will ensure, with the support of the students, parents and community, that all students, upon graduation, will have the academic and social skills to be successful, lifelong learners in a global society.</p>

<div class="mobile-header-bar"><div class="mobile-navigation"><a class="dt-mobile-menu-icon floating-btn" href="#"><span class="lines"></span></a><a class="dt-mobile-menu-icon" href="#"><span class="lines"></span></a></div><div class="mobile-mini-widgets"><div class="text-area in-menu-on-mobile"><h1 class="mission-header"><span>Our Mission</span></h1>
<div class="mission-header">
<p>The mission of the Rockford Public Schools is to provide a safe teaching and learning environment which will ensure, with the support of the students, parents and community, that all students, upon graduation, will have the academic and social skills to be successful, lifelong learners in a global society.</p>
</div>

<div class="mobile-mini-widgets-in-menu"><div class="text-area in-menu-on-mobile"><h1 class="mission-header"><span>Our Mission</span></h1>
<div class="mission-header">
<p>The mission of the Rockford Public Schools is to provide a safe teaching and learning environment which will ensure, with the support of the students, parents and community, that all students, upon graduation, will have the academic and social skills to be successful, lifelong learners in a global society.</p>
</div>
</div></div>

<div class="wpb_wrapper">
<h1 class="titletextpage" style="text-align: center; font-size: 44px;">Rockford Public Schools Mission Statement</h1>
</div>
</div>
<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element vc_custom_1538537964081">
<div class="wpb_wrapper">
<h3 style="text-align: center; max-width: 85%; margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto;">The mission of the Rockford Public Schools is to provide a safe teaching and learning environment which will ensure, with the support of the students, parents and community, that all students, upon graduation, will have the academic and social skills to be successful, lifelong learners in a global society.</h3>
</div>

<div id="lipsum">
<h2 style="text-align: left;">Blue Ribbon Exemplary Schools</h2>
<p>All schools in Rockford School District are Blue Ribbon Exemplary Schools and are fully accredited through AdvancED</p>
<h2 style="text-align: left;"></h2>
</div>

<h2 style="text-align: left;">Blue Ribbon Exemplary Schools</h2>
<p>All schools in Rockford School District are Blue Ribbon Exemplary Schools and are fully accredited through AdvancED</p>

<div class="blog-media wf-td"><p><a class="alignnone rollover this-ready material-click-effect ripplelink" href="https://www.schoolnewsnetwork.org/2019/10/04/state-superintendent-tours-classrooms-and-new-learning-center/" style="padding-bottom: 75%; height: 0px; display: block;" target="_blank"><span class="ink" style="height: 155px; width: 155px;"></span><img alt="" class="preload-me retinized height-ready" data-ratio="1.3333333333333333" height="522" src="http://www.rockfordschools.org/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Supt-Rice.jpg" srcset="http://www.rockfordschools.org/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Supt-Rice.jpg 1x, http://www.rockfordschools.org/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Supt-Rice.jpg 2x" width="696"/><i></i></a></p></div><div class="blog-content wf-td">
<h3 class="entry-title">
<a href="https://www.schoolnewsnetwork.org/2019/10/04/state-superintendent-tours-classrooms-and-new-learning-center/" rel="bookmark" target="_blank" title="State Superintendent Rice tours RHS and Roguewood Elementary">State Superintendent Rice tours RHS and Roguewood Elementary</a>
</h3>
<p>Students and staff at Roguewood Elementary School and Rockford High School recently got an encouraging visit from the top education official in Michigan, State Superintendent Michael Rice. Rice visited several…</p>
<a class="details more-link" href="https://www.schoolnewsnetwork.org/2019/10/04/state-superintendent-tours-classrooms-and-new-learning-center/" rel="nofollow" target="_blank">Details</a>

					//JOSH'S NEW CODE LOGIC 
					var startMonth = parseInt(start_date_arr[0]);
					//Subtract 1 from month because it goes from 0-11 not 1-12
					startMonth -= 1;
					
					var startDay = parseInt(start_date_arr[1]);
					var startYear = parseInt(start_date_arr[2]);
					var startDate = new Date(startYear, startMonth, startDay);
					
					var endMonth = parseInt(end_date_arr[0]);
					//Subtract 1 from month because it goes from 0-11 not 1-12
					endMonth -= 1;
					
					var endDay = parseInt(end_date_arr[1]);
					var endYear = parseInt(end_date_arr[2]);

					//Subtract 1 from month because it goes from 0-11 not 1-12
					month -= 1;

					var todaysDate = new Date(year, month, today);
					
					
					if((todaysDate <= endDate && todaysDate >= startDate))
					{
						start_date_in_date_range = true; 
						end_date_in_date_range = true;
					}
					
					//start times
					/* TIM'S OLD CODE LOGIC
					if(parseInt(today_arr[2]) > parseInt(start_date_arr[2]))
					{

					}
					else if(parseInt(today_arr[2]) == parseInt(start_date_arr[2]) && parseInt(today_arr[0]) > parseInt(start_date_arr[0]))

					{
						start_date_in_date_range = true;
					}
					else if(parseInt(today_arr[2]) == parseInt(start_date_arr[2]) && parseInt(today_arr[0]) == parseInt(start_date_arr[0])
						&& parseInt(today_arr[1]) == parseInt(start_date_arr[1]) && hour == start_hour && min >= start_min)
					{
						start_date_in_date_range = true;
					}
					
					//end times

											start_hour += 12;
										}
										else if(start_am == "AM" && start_hour == 12)
										{
											start_hour = 0;
										}
										
										if(end_am == "PM")
										{
											end_hour += 12;
										}
										else if(end_am == "AM" && end_hour == 12)
										{
											end_hour = 0;
										}
										
										
										if(todaysDate <= endDate && todaysDate >= startDate)
										{
											//console.log("??");
											var addMessage = true;
											if(todaysDate.getTime() == endDate.getTime() && currentHour <= end_hour)
											{
												if(currentHour == end_hour)
												{
													if(currentMinute <= end_min)
													{
														console.log("within date range hour");
														addMessage = true;
													}
													else 
													{
														console.log("not in date range");
														addMessage = false;
													}
												}
												else
												{
													addMessage = true;
													console.log("within date range");
												}
											}
											if(todaysDate.getTime() == startDate.getTime() && currentHour >= start_hour)
											{
												
												if(currentHour == start_hour)
												{
													if(currentMinute >= start_min)
													{
														console.log("within date range hour");	
														addMessage = true;
													}
													else
													{
														console.log("NOT IN THE DATE RANGE");
														addMessage = false;
													}
												}
												else
												{
													console.log("within date range normal");
													addMessage = true;
												}
												
											}
											
											/*
											console.log("starting date = " + startDate.getTime());
											console.log("Todays date = " + todaysDate.getTime());
											console.log("currentHour = " + currentHour);
											console.log("currentMinute = " + currentMinute);
											console.log("startingHour = " + start_hour);
											console.log("startignMin = " + start_min);
											*/
											
											start_date_in_date_range = true; 
											end_date_in_date_range = true;
											if(addMessage)
											{
												messageText += '<h2 style=\"text-align: center;\">Varsity Football<br \/>Rockford at Grandville<\/h2>\r\n<h3 style=\"text-align: center;\">Friday, October 18<br \/>Kick-Off is at <span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">4:30 p.m.<\/span><\/h3>';
											}
										}
									}
									else
									{
										messageText += '<h2 style=\"text-align: center;\">Varsity Football<br \/>Rockford at Grandville<\/h2>\r\n<h3 style=\"text-align: center;\">Friday, October 18<br \/>Kick-Off is at <span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\">4:30 p.m.<\/span><\/h3>';
									}
															console.log(messageText);
						if(messageText.length > 1)
						{
							jQuery.fancybox.open({
								afterClose: function() {
									
								   /* jQuery.cookie('popup', 'true', {
										expires: 1
									});*/
									
								 },
								content:
								'<div class="popup curvingBorder color2" style="padding:30px; font-size:15px; min-height:200px; max-width:500px;">'+messageText+
																										'</div>'
								
							});
						}
					});
					
				}, 3000);
				
				
			}
	
			
	</script>
<script>
		
		jQuery(document).ready(function() {
			
			var url_day_get_data = "";
			var url_day_cal = "http://www.rockfordschools.org/day-calendar/?current_date=";
			
			jQuery('.next_month').click(function(){
				
				var url_data_from_link = jQuery('.to_day_view').attr("href");
				var temp_Get_holder ="";
				url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link.split('?');
				if(jQuery(url_data_from_link).size() > 1)
				{
					url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link[1];
					url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link.split('&');
					url_day_get_data = "";
					
					for(var i = 0; i < jQuery(url_data_from_link).size(); i++)
					{
						temp_Get_holder = url_data_from_link[i].split('=');
						
						if(jQuery(temp_Get_holder).size() > 1)
						{
							if(temp_Get_holder[0] == "fac")
							{
								url_day_get_data += "&fac="+temp_Get_holder[1]+""
							}
							else if(temp_Get_holder[0] == "cat")
							{
								url_day_get_data += "&cat="+temp_Get_holder[1]+""
							}
						}
						
					}
					
				}
				else
				{
					url_data_from_link = "";
				}
				
				month_id = '#month_'+active_month+'_'+active_year;
				//jQuery(month_id).hide();
				jQuery(month_id).addClass("hide_jcm_cal");
				active_month++;
				
				if(active_month == 13)
				{
					active_month = 1;
					active_year++;
				}
				
				day_view_link = url_day_cal+''+active_month+"-1-"+active_year+""+url_day_get_data;
				jQuery('.to_day_view').attr("href", day_view_link);
				month_id = '#month_'+active_month+'_'+active_year;
				//jQuery(month_id).show();
				jQuery(month_id).removeClass("hide_jcm_cal");
	
			});
			
			jQuery('.last_month').click(function(){
				
				var url_data_from_link = jQuery('.to_day_view').attr("href");
				var temp_Get_holder ="";
				url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link.split('?');
				if(jQuery(url_data_from_link).size() > 1)
				{
					url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link[1];
					url_data_from_link = url_data_from_link.split('&');
					url_day_get_data = "";
					
					for(var i = 0; i < jQuery(url_data_from_link).size(); i++)
					{
						temp_Get_holder = url_data_from_link[i].split('=');
						
						if(jQuery(temp_Get_holder).size() > 1)
						{
							if(temp_Get_holder[0] == "fac")
							{
								url_day_get_data += "&fac="+temp_Get_holder[1]+""
							}
							else if(temp_Get_holder[0] == "cat")
							{
								url_day_get_data += "&cat="+temp_Get_holder[1]+""
							}
						}
						
					}
					
				}
				else
				{
					url_data_from_link = "";
				}
				
				month_id = '#month_'+active_month+'_'+active_year;
				//jQuery(month_id).hide();
				jQuery(month_id).addClass("hide_jcm_cal");
				active_month--;
				
				if(active_month == 0)
				{
					active_month = 12;
					active_year--;
				}
				
				day_view_link = url_day_cal+''+active_month+"-1-"+active_year+""+url_day_get_data;
				jQuery('.to_day_view').attr("href", day_view_link);
				month_id = '#month_'+active_month+'_'+active_year;
				//jQuery(month_id).show();
				jQuery(month_id).removeClass("hide_jcm_cal");
	
			});
			
			jQuery('.to_previous_page').click(function(){
				parent.history.back();
				return false;
			});
			
			jQuery('.jcm_ds_item').click(function(){
				
				var info_id = this.id;
				var info_id = info_id.split('_');
				var show_this_id = "#"+info_id[0]+"_box_"+info_id[3];
				
				jQuery('.jcm_ds_box').hide();
				//jQuery(show_this_id).show();
				
				/*jQuery('.jcm_ds_box').animate({
				    opacity: 1,
				    left: "+=50",
				    height: "toggle"
				  }, 1000, function() {
				    // Animation complete.
				  });*/
				
				jQuery(show_this_id).animate({
				    opacity: 1,
				    left: "+=50",
				    height: "toggle"
				  }, 1000, function() {
				    // Animation complete.
				  });
			
				jQuery('.jcm_picked').addClass("jcm_not_picked");
				jQuery('.jcm_picked').removeClass("jcm_picked");
				jQuery(this).addClass("jcm_picked");
	
			});
			
								
				jQuery(".sort_schools").change(function() {
					var rel_url = "http://www.rockfordschools.org/";
					
					if(jQuery(".sort_schools").val() != "")
					{
						rel_url += jQuery(".sort_schools").val();
					}
					
					jQuery(".jcm_fac_sort_button").attr("href", rel_url);
				});
				
				if(jQuery(window).width() < 600)
				{
					if(jQuery('.jcm_to_month_view').length)
					{
						jQuery('.jcm_to_month_view').hide();
					}
					/*else
					{
						console.log("false");
					}*/
					
				}
				
		});

	jQuery(document).ready(function() {
			
		function submenu_toggle_1(passed_this)
		{
			submenu_id = passed_this.id.split('_');
			submenu_id = submenu_id[1]
			
			if(sub_menu_show == true)
			{
				if(jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).is(':visible'))
				{
					sub_menu_show = false;
					animate_page(browser, false);
					
					jQuery('.jcm_tablist').animate({
			  
					  //'margin-right' : "-=19%"
					 'margin-right' : "-=360px"
					
					});
					
					jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
					
					jQuery('.jcm_submenu').animate({
			  
					  'right' : "-=372px"
					
					});
					
					setTimeout(function(){
					 	 jQuery(".jcm_submenu").hide();
					}, 250);
					
					jQuery('.active_tab').removeClass('active_tab');
					
					if(hamburger_click == false)
					{
						/*jQuery('.static_icons_section').animate({
	
					  		'right' : "-=360px"
					  		
						});*/
						
						/*jQuery('.exit_out').animate({
	
					 		'right' : "-=360px"
					
						});*/
					}
					
					jQuery('.jcm_tablist').addClass('jcm_css_animate');
						
				}
				else
				{
					jQuery('.active_tab').removeClass('active_tab');
					jQuery(passed_this).find('span').addClass('active_tab');
					
					bg_color = jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).css('background-color');
					jQuery('body').css('background-color',bg_color);
					
					jQuery(".jcm_submenu").hide();
					jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).show();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				sub_menu_show = true;
				jQuery('.jcm_css_animate').removeClass('jcm_css_animate');
				
				jQuery(passed_this).find('span').addClass('active_tab');
				
				if(jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).is(':visible'))
				{
					jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).hide;
				}
				else
				{
					bg_color = jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).css('background-color');
					jQuery('body').css('background-color',bg_color);
					jQuery('#submenu_'+submenu_id).show();
				}
				
				jQuery('.jcm_tablist').animate({
				  
				  //'margin-right' : "+=19%"
				  'margin-right' : "+=360px"
				
				});
				jQuery('.jcm_submenu').animate({
				  
				  'right' : "+=372px"
				
				});
				
				animate_page(browser, true);
				
				if(hamburger_click == false)
				{
					/*jQuery('.static_icons_section').animate({

				  		'right' : "+=360px"
				
					});*/
					
					/*jQuery('.exit_out').animate({

				 		 'right' : "+=360px"
				
					});*/
				}
				
			}
		}	
			
		jQuery('.show_submenu').keypress(function(e) {

		    }
		});

		jQuery('body').click(function(evt){    
			
			if(sub_menu_show == true)

		       	sub_menu_show = false;

				jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');  
				jQuery('.jcm_tablist').addClass('jcm_css_animate');

		});
		//== For closing submenu when clicking elsewhere code End ==
		
		// Start code for closing side navigation when going to another page
		jQuery(window).bind('beforeunload', function(){
			
			if(!(navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Safari') != -1 && navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Chrome') == -1))
			{
				if(hide == true)
				{
					if(page_away_ham_stay != true)
					{
						jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').animate({
		
						  /* was'margin-left' : "+=320px"*/
						  'margin-right' : "-=320px"
						
						});
						
						keep_tabs_out = false;
						jQuery('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-bars');
						//jQuery('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-mouse-pointer');
						jQuery('.hamburger_icon').removeClass('fa-close');
					}
				}
				else if(hide == false)
				{
					if(sub_menu_show == true)
					{
						if(page_away_ham_stay == true)
						{
							page_away = true;
						}
						
						sub_menu_show = false
					  	animate_page(browser, false);
							
						jQuery('.jcm_tablist').animate({
				  
						  //'margin-right' : "-=69%"
						  'margin-right' : "-=800px"
						
						});
						
						jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
						
						jQuery('.jcm_submenu').animate({
				  
						  'right' : "-=372px"
						
						});
						
						if(page_away_ham_stay != true)
						{
							jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').animate({
		
							  /*'margin-left' : "+=320px"*/
							  'margin-right' : "-=320px"
							
							});
							
							keep_tabs_out = false;
							jQuery('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-bars');
							//jQuery('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-mouse-pointer');
							jQuery('.hamburger_icon').removeClass('fa-close');
						}
						
						jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
						
						setTimeout(function(){
						 	 jQuery(".jcm_submenu").hide();
						 	 //hover_effects();
						 	 jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
						}, 250);
						
						if(hamburger_click == false)
						{
							
							/*jQuery('.static_icons_section').animate({
	
						  		'right' : "-=760px"
						
							});*/
							
							/*jQuery('.exit_out').animate({
	
						 		'right' : "-=760px"
						
							});*/
						}
						
						jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
						
						
					}
					else if(sub_menu_show == false)
					{
						if(page_away_ham_stay == true)
						{
							page_away = true;
						}
						
					  	jQuery('.jcm_tablist').animate({
				  
						  //'margin-right' : "-=50%"
						  'margin-right' : "-400px"
						
						});
						
						if(page_away_ham_stay != true)
						{
							jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').animate({
		
							  /*was 'margin-left' : "+=320px"*/
							  'margin-right' : "-=320px"
							
							});
							
							keep_tabs_out = false;
							jQuery('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-bars');
							//Query('.hamburger_icon').addClass('fa-mouse-pointer');
							jQuery('.hamburger_icon').removeClass('fa-close');
						}
						
						if(hamburger_click == false)
						{
							/*jQuery('.static_icons_section').animate({
	
						  		'right' : "-=760px"
						
							});*/
							
							/*jQuery('.exit_out').animate({
	
						 		 'right' : "-=760px"
						
							});*/
						}
					}
				}
			}
		});
		//End code for closing side navigation when going to another page
		
		jQuery( window ).resize(function() {
			
			currentWidth = jQuery(window).width();
			
			if(start_mobile == true && currentWidth > 1110)
			{
				//Comment out to prevent page form refreshing on resize
				//window.location.replace(window.location.href);
			}
			else if(sub_menu_show == true && currentWidth <= 1110)
			{
		       	sub_menu_show = false;
		       	
		       	animate_page(browser, false);
		       	
				jQuery('.jcm_tablist').animate({
		  
				  //'margin-right' : "-=19%"
				  'margin-right' : "-=360px"
				
				});
				
				jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
				
				jQuery('.jcm_submenu').animate({
		  
				  'right' : "-=372px"
				
				});
				
				setTimeout(function(){
				 	 jQuery(".jcm_submenu").hide();
				 	 jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
				}, 250);  
				
				jQuery('.active_tab').removeClass('active_tab');
				
				if(hamburger_click == false)
				{
					/*jQuery('.static_icons_section').animate({

				  		'right' : "-=360px"
				
					});*/
					
					/*jQuery('.exit_out').animate({

				 		'right' : "-=360px"
				
					});*/
					
					jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
				}  
				
				jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');  
				jQuery('.jcm_tablist').addClass('jcm_css_animate'); 
		
		    }
		    
		    if(start_mobile == false && currentWidth <= 1470)
			{
				start_mobile = true;
			}
			
		});
		
		//Fix hamburger bug hopelly
		
		jQuery(document).ready(function() {
			setTimeout(function()
			{ 
				if(jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').css("margin-right") == "320px")
				{
					//console.log("Had to run that hamburger code");
					jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').css("margin-right","0px");
					jQuery('.jcm_tablist').css("margin-right","-400px");
				}
				
			}, 1000);
			
		});
		
	</script>

$form = jQuery('.w-form'),
$btnOpen = jQuery( ".w-search-open" );
$btnClose = jQuery('.close_style');
$input = $form.find('.search_feild');
$overlay = jQuery('.w-search-background');
$window = jQuery(window);
searchOverlayInitRadius = 25;
showHideTimer = null;

jQuery( ".w-search-open" ).click(function() {
  	jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
        jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').css('z-index','100');
	jQuery( ".w-search-background" ).show();

var searchPos = $btnOpen.offset(),
	searchWidth = $btnOpen.width(),
	searchHeight = $btnOpen.height();

	// Preserving scroll position
	searchPos.top -= $window.scrollTop();
	searchPos.left -= $window.scrollLeft();
	var overlayX = searchPos.left+searchWidth/2,
	overlayY = searchPos.top+searchHeight/2,
	//winWidth = $us.canvas.winWidth,
	//winHeight = $us.canvas.winHeight,
	winWidth = jQuery(window).width();
	winHeight = jQuery(window).height();

	// Counting distance to the nearest screen corner
	overlayRadius = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(Math.max(winWidth - overlayX, overlayX), 2) + Math.pow(Math.max(winHeight - overlayY, overlayY), 2)),
	overlayScale = (overlayRadius+15)/searchOverlayInitRadius;

	$overlay.css({
		width: searchOverlayInitRadius*2,
		height: searchOverlayInitRadius*2,
		left: overlayX,
		top: overlayY,
		"margin-left": -searchOverlayInitRadius,
		"margin-top": -searchOverlayInitRadius
	});

	$overlay
		.removeClass('overlay-out')
		.show();

	$form.css({
		opacity: 0,
		display: 'block',
		'-webkit-transition': 'opacity 0.4s 0.3s',
		transition: 'opacity 0.4s 0.3s'
	});

	window.setTimeout(function()
	{
		$overlay
			.addClass('overlay-on')
			.css({
				"-webkit-transform": "scale(" + overlayScale + ")",
				"transform": "scale(" + overlayScale + ")"
			});

		$form.css('opacity', 1);
		clearInterval(showHideTimer);

		showHideTimer = window.setTimeout(function() 
		{
			$input.focus();
			//jQuery(".search_feild").focus();
		}, 700);
	}, 25);


});

//Closing Search overlay button start

jQuery($btnClose).click(function() {
	jQuery('.rev_slider_wrapper').css('left','0px');
	$input.blur();
	$form.css({
		'-webkit-transition': 'opacity 0.4s',
		transition: 'opacity 0.4s'
	});

	window.setTimeout(function(){
		$overlay
			.removeClass('overlay-on')
			.addClass('overlay-out')

			.css({
				'-webkit-transform': 'scale(0.1)',
				'transform': 'scale(0.1)'
			});

		$form.css('opacity', 0);
		clearTimeout(showHideTimer);
		showHideTimer = window.setTimeout(function(){
			$form.css('display', 'none');
			$overlay.css('display', 'none');
			jQuery('.jcm_hamburger').css('z-index','101');
		}, 700);
	}, 25);

});

//Closing Search overlay button end

function scrollToAnchor(aid){
	//console.log("Start scrollToanchor function");
    var aTag = jQuery("a[name='"+ aid +"']");
    jQuery('html,body').animate({scrollTop: aTag.offset().top},'slow');
	//console.log("End scrollToAnchor function");
}

jQuery(".scrolling_click").click(function() {
	//console.log("Id "+this.id)
	scrollToAnchor(this.id);
});

/*--- End Animate down to an element code---*/

jQuery(document).ready(function() {
  jQuery('.search_class').on('change', function() {
    var $form = jQuery(this).closest('form');
    $form.find('input[type=submit]').click();
  });

  var staff_websites = jQuery(".no_website");

  for(var i = 0; i < staff_websites.size(); i++)
  {
	if(jQuery(staff_websites[i]).attr("href") == "")
	{
		jQuery(staff_websites[i]).remove();
	}
  }

  jQuery('img').removeAttr('title');

});

jQuery(document).ready(function() {

	var heading = '';
	var grid_p =  '';

setTimeout(function()
{ 
	//console.log("Running space code.");
	for(var i = 0; i < jQuery('.vc_custom_heading p').length; i++)
	{
		heading = jQuery('.vc_custom_heading p');
		heading = heading[i];
		
		newStr = jQuery(heading).text();
		
		newStr = newStr.replace(/&nbsp;/g,'');
		newStr = newStr.replace(/ +(?= )/g,'');
		newStr = newStr.replace(/\s+/g,' ');
		//newStr = newStr.replace(/  +/g, ' ');

		jQuery(heading).text(newStr);
		
	}

}, 3500);


});

<script type="text/javascript">
var renderInvisibleReCaptcha = function() {

    for (var i = 0; i < document.forms.length; ++i) {
        var form = document.forms[i];
        var holder = form.querySelector('.inv-recaptcha-holder');

        if (null === holder) continue;
		holder.innerHTML = '';

         (function(frm){
			var cf7SubmitElm = frm.querySelector('.wpcf7-submit');
            var holderId = grecaptcha.render(holder,{
                'sitekey': '6Le4jJcUAAAAABKHQ4XLnGVHi1p1WEtL5iJZHa3T', 'size': 'invisible', 'badge' : 'bottomright',
                'callback' : function (recaptchaToken) {
					if((null !== cf7SubmitElm) && (typeof jQuery != 'undefined')){jQuery(frm).submit();grecaptcha.reset(holderId);return;}
					 HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(frm);
                },
                'expired-callback' : function(){grecaptcha.reset(holderId);}
            });

			if(null !== cf7SubmitElm && (typeof jQuery != 'undefined') ){
				jQuery(cf7SubmitElm).off('click').on('click', function(clickEvt){
					clickEvt.preventDefault();
					grecaptcha.execute(holderId);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				frm.onsubmit = function (evt){evt.preventDefault();grecaptcha.execute(holderId);};
			}


        })(form);
    }
};
</script>
